# Miziki

A local-first music player for iPhone (installed from Safari to the home screen), served from GitHub Pages at
https://esang-mao.github.io/Miziki/miziki.html. It plays FLAC, WAV, ALAC and MP3 files the user picks from their device. Nothing is uploaded. An optional social layer (Supabase) sits on top, and the player must work fully without it.

Long-term plan: modularize the codebase, build out the Supabase social layer, then wrap the app with Capacitor for the App Store.

## Repo layout

- `miziki.html` holds the markup. The main player script used to be one big inline `<script>` block at the end of `<body>`; it now lives in `src/main.js` (step 2a), loaded via `<script type="module" src="src/main.js">` in that same position. It is still one big ~10,100-line file — splitting *that* up is the rest of step 2 and beyond. See "Refactor rules" below. Since step 01 (security hardening), its `<head>` also carries the Content Security Policy and `referrer` meta tags — see "Security" below; GitHub Pages can't set real HTTP response headers, so the CSP has to live here as a `<meta http-equiv>` tag, not a header. Since step D2, `<head>` also loads `src/theme-boot.js` (a classic `<script src>`, placed before the stylesheet `<link>` tags so it blocks parsing and sets the `data-theme` attribute before any CSS applies — see that file's own entry) and carries the Character-menu Appearance row's markup (three `[data-theme-choice]` buttons) in the Character route. Since step D3 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), the tab bar (`<nav id="tabbar">`, unchanged) is wrapped in a new `<div class="sidebar" id="sidebar">`, physically still in the exact same spot right after the mini-player, with new sibling markup inside that same wrapper (`<div class="sidebar-extra" id="sidebarExtra">`: `#sidebarPlaylists`, the playlists list `src/shell/sidebar.js` renders into, and `#sidebarSundown`'s `#sideRateWhy`/`#sideLegStart`/`#sideLegEnd`, which `player/clock.js`'s `drawSun()` mirrors into) — invisible scaffolding below the 1024px breakpoint (`src/styles/23-desktop.css`'s `.sidebar{display:contents}`), the desktop left sidebar above it. Since step D5, `#libNav` (the Library route's own view-mode tab row) also carries the new desktop-only crate order toggle's markup, `<div class="seg" id="crateGroupToggleTab">` (two `[data-group]` buttons, `az`/`genre`), inserted right before the existing `#hdrActions` — new ids, the same "mirror existing state, don't clone or relocate the control" shape the sidebar's own playlists list and the D4 player bar's controls already established; the existing Profile → Library tools copy, `#crateGroupToggle`, is untouched. Since step D6, the full-player overlay (`#playerOverlay`) gained a new `.player-layout` wrapper around the existing `.stage` (itself gaining a new `#stageInfo` wrapper around six pre-existing, untouched elements — `.now`/`#scrub`/`.times`/`.transport`/`.modes`/`#modeNote`) and a sibling `#playerSidePanel` (three tabs, `#playerSideTabs`, over one body, `#playerSideBody`) — desktop-only, invisible scaffolding below the breakpoint the same way D3's sidebar and D5's crate detail panel already are; no existing id moved, removed, or changed.
- `src/main.js` (step 7f) is now the short boot file the "wiring" header always implied it should become — everything else named in this bullet through step 6 has moved out into its own module. It imports just what the boot sequence itself needs, calls each of the 13 `wireX()` functions from `src/wiring/` (below) once, then runs the original startup sequence verbatim: `renderSearchChips()`, the 13 wire calls, `setMode('vinyl')`/`fallbackSun()`/`askLocation()`/`drawSun()`/`updateChromeInset()` plus its `resize` listener, `requestAnimationFrame(frame)`, then `restoreLibrary()` (unchanged, still the boot sequence's own async function — opens storage, loads tracks, restores meta/sessions/seals, rebuilds the people index and crate model, renders the library, heals thumbnails/details/crate art in the background, marks the library ready for the launch intro, sets the initial queue to the last-played album/track, and inits `MizikiSocial`, including the dynamic `?social=demo` import) and the launch-intro gate. Since step 06 (B3), also calls `initBackupSync()` and wires the one `onBackup('applied', ...)` listener (the only place outside `backup.js` itself that reacts to a merged pull — see `sync/backup.js`'s own entry for why `backup.js` can't safely import `renderTracks`/`applyDiscVariant`/`renderProfile` directly): `renderTracks()`, `applyDiscVariant(current(), true)` if a track is loaded, and, since step 07 (B4, for a downloaded cover/avatar to actually show up on the currently-open profile tab), `renderProfile()` if `isProfileRouteVisible()` — all three imported for real (`library/view.js`, `record-art/looks.js`, `profile/screens.js`, `shell/routes.js`) specifically because `main.js` is the one place in the app that can safely hold all of them at once (`REDUCED || document.hidden` skips straight to removing `#launchOverlay`; otherwise `runLaunchSequence()`). A module now, not a classic script: nothing it declares at the top level is a property of `window` (confirmed nothing relies on that — see the step 2a PR). `src/miziki-social.js` stays a classic script loaded just before it, so `MizikiSocial` is already a real global by the time this runs.
- `src/wiring/` (step 7f, plus `lifecycle.js` in step 06/B3) — the "wiring" IIFE's own ~430 lines, split into one file per UI area, each exporting a single `wireX()` function that attaches event listeners and nothing else. Every function any of the 13 step-7f files calls was already a real export of its owning module before that step — so, unlike every other step 7 PR, none of them needed a single new export added anywhere else in the codebase, and none of them import from `main.js` (rule 8's list stays exactly as empty as it was after step 7e). Two deliberate groupings, each a judgment call since step 7f had no file-list table to follow (the step 7 plan only names the area as a whole): `account.js` combines the profile-picture picker with the social sign-in/nav-stack close buttons, since both are small "identity" concerns and neither is big enough alone to warrant its own file; `routes.js` is kept separate from `library-view.js` (rather than folded in, even though both are tab-switching) since the top-level route tab bar is app-wide navigation, not a library-screen concern. The global Escape-key handler (gatefold → sealed sheet → add menu → search field, in that priority order) lives in `gatefold.js` rather than its own file, since the gatefold and sealed sheet are the first two things it closes.
  - `lifecycle.js` (step 06, B3) — `wireLifecycle()`: the one new wiring file this step adds, since it's a new UI-adjacent concern (backgrounding/foregrounding), not an extraction from `main.js`. Listens for `visibilitychange` (hidden → `flushBackupSync()`, so a backgrounded or closed tab doesn't lose up to 30s of unsynced changes; visible again → `pullBackupOnForeground()`, so a device picks up what another device backed up while this one was away) and `pagehide` (`flushBackupSync()` again, as a second safety net — iOS Safari fires `pagehide`, not always a reliable `visibilitychange`, when a home-screen app is actually terminated). Imports `flushBackupSync`/`pullBackupOnForeground` from `../sync/backup.js` — no `main.js` import. Both calls are fire-and-forget: neither is awaited, and neither can block or throw into the event loop (see `src/sync/backup.js`'s own entry below for why that's safe).
  - `transport.js` — `wireTransport()`: play/pause (including the `bgPrime()`/`ensureContext()`/pull-ceremony logic `#play` and `#miniPlay` both run), next/prev, shuffle/repeat, the scrub slider, `wireSpinToScrub()`, the Pure/Vinyl mode buttons, the sleep timer open/close, the player's and mini-player's favorite buttons, the `visibilitychange` listener that calls `touchActiveSession()` on backgrounding, the mini-player tap-to-open, and the player-overlay pointerdown that interrupts whichever in-flight ceremony (start sequence, morph, gatefold hand-off) is running. Imports `S`/`current`, `$`, `bgPrime` (`player/background.js`), `ensureContext` (`audio/engine.js`), `pause`/`play`/`seek` (`player/transport.js`), `nextTrack`/`prevTrack`/`toggleShuffle`/`cycleRepeat` (`player/controls.js`), `setMode` (`audio/mode.js`), `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`), `wireSpinToScrub` (`player/scrub.js`), `openSleepSheet`/`closeSleepSheet` (`player/sleep-timer.js`), `toggleFavorite` (`library/favorites.js`), `touchActiveSession` (`history/sessions.js`), `updateShellAlbum` (`shell/mini-player.js`), `openPlayer`/`closePlayer`/`morphInFlight`/`interruptMorph` (`shell/player-overlay.js`), `runStartSequence`/`interruptStartSequence` (`shell/start-sequence.js`), `gfHandoffInFlight`/`interruptGatefoldHandoff` (`shell/handoff.js`) — no `main.js` import. Since step D6 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), also wires the desktop-only side panel's three tabs (`#playerSideTabs [data-panel-tab]`): each click flips `aria-selected` on the clicked button and calls `renderPlayerSidePanel` (`shell/player-side-panel.js`), plus one initial call at the end of `wireTransport()` itself, the same "initial call" shape `wireCharacter()`'s own `bgUpdateUI()` call already has.
  - `library-view.js` — `wireLibraryView()`: the Songs/Artists/Playlists/Crate view-mode tabs, the library layout toggle, the crate group toggle, the album-sides/sealed-records/play-in-full-player toggles, the library tools disclosure panel (S1 §5), select mode (toggle/cancel/edit/number/delete), the row overflow menu, and the shared confirm-delete dialog. Imports `S`, `$`, `idxOf` (`library/model.js`), `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`), `invalidateCrateModel` (`crate/model.js`), `renderTracks`/`enterSelectMode`/`exitSelectMode`/`closeRowMenu`/`openConfirmDelete`/`closeConfirmDelete`/`rowMenuTrackId`/`confirmDeleteAction`/`applyTrackNumbersInOrder` (`library/view.js`), `deleteTracks` (`library/delete.js`), `openEditSheet` (`library/edit.js`), `openCreditsSheet` (`library/credits.js`), `toggleFavorite` (`library/favorites.js`), `playNext`/`addToQueue` (`player/queue.js`) — no `main.js` import. Since step D5, the existing crate group toggle's click wiring also covers the new desktop-only tab-row copy (`#crateGroupToggleTab [data-group]`) in the same `querySelectorAll` call as the original (`#crateGroupToggle [data-group]`) — one combined selector, since both copies drive the identical `S.crateGroup` write/`invalidateCrateModel()`/`queueSave()`/`renderTracks()` sequence; no new import needed for this.
  - `search.js` — `wireSearch()`: the library search field's own input/focus/blur handling (the 120ms debounce into `renderTracks`) and the header search icon/Cancel/× (S1 §2.1). Imports `$`, `renderTracks` (`library/view.js`), `updateSearchChipsVisibility` (`library/search.js`), `openSearchField`/`closeSearchField` (`shell/header.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `import.js` — `wireImport()`: the header `+` menu's Add songs/Add folder rows (S1 §3), the file pickers and test-tone buttons, drag-and-drop over the library route (`filesFromDataTransferItems`), the single-file duplicate prompt's four buttons, and the library-wide duplicate scan's open/close. Imports `$`, `addFiles`/`filesFromDataTransferItems`/`addTestTone` (`library/import.js`), `closeDupOverlay`/`closeDuplicateScan` (`library/duplicates-ui.js`), `openDuplicateScan` (`library/duplicate-scan.js`), `openAddMenu`/`closeAddMenu`/`isAddMenuOpen` (`shell/header.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `library-management.js` — `wireLibraryManagement()`: the "Forget library" two-tap arm/confirm button and the clear-listening-history overlay (orphaned-only vs. everything). Imports `$`, `forgetLibrary` (`library/delete.js`), `clearListeningHistory` (`history/clear.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `gatefold.js` — `wireGatefold()`: the global Escape-key handler described above, plus the gatefold's own back/panel-switch/resize controls and the sealed-record sheet's close button. Imports `$`, `isGatefoldOpen`/`closeGatefold`/`gatefoldSwitchPanel`/`updateGatefoldScale` (`gatefold/view.js`), `isSealedSheetOpen`/`closeSealedSheet` (`gatefold/sealed-sheet.js`), `isAddMenuOpen`/`closeAddMenu`/`isSearchFieldOpen`/`closeSearchField` (`shell/header.js`), `renderTracks` (`library/view.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `sundown.js` — `wireSundown()`: the auto/manual toggle, the manual-rate slider, the full-night-rate target buttons, the pacing buttons, and the preview slider plus its Live button. Imports `S`, `$`, `nowClock` (`sundown/solar.js`), `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `character.js` — `wireCharacter()`: the character sliders and presets, the Scratch toggle, Car Mode's motion toggle, sleeve pull, launch intro (including the `attachLaunchSettingRow()` call), background playback (including the initial `bgUpdateUI()` call), and the Car Mode floor/max-speed sliders. Since step D2, also the Appearance row's three `[data-theme-choice]` buttons (`document.querySelectorAll` rather than fixed ids, so the desktop sidebar's own control, D7, needs no change here once it shares the same attribute). Imports `S`, `$`, `applyCharacter` (`audio/engine.js`), `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`), `setMode` (`audio/mode.js`), `toggleScratch` (`player/scrub.js`), `toggleMotion`/`toggleSleevePull` (`sundown/location.js`), `toggleLaunchIntro`/`attachLaunchSettingRow` (`launch/intro.js`), `toggleBgAudio`/`bgUpdateUI` (`player/background.js`), and, since step D2, `setTheme` (`ui/theme.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `edit.js` — `wireEdit()`: the edit sheet (open from the player, close/save/revert, the art picker's crop-then-preview, undo) and the credits sheet (open from the player, close). Imports `S`/`current`, `$`, `cropSquareImage`/`revertTrack`/`undoLastEdit`/`openEditSheet`/`closeEditSheet`/`saveEditSheet`/`renderEditArtPreview` (`library/edit.js`), `openCreditsSheet`/`closeCreditsSheet` (`library/credits.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `account.js` — `wireAccount()`: the profile-picture picker (crop to 500px, persist, re-render), the social sign-in sheet's close button, the social nav stack's back button, and the record sheet's close button — see the grouping rationale above. Imports `S`, `$`, `persistProfile`/`renderProfile` (`profile/screens.js`), `closeSocialAuth` (`profile/settings.js`), `popSocialScreen`/`closeRecordSheet` (`social/screens/friend-store.js`), `cropSquareImage` (`library/edit.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `share.js` — `wireShare()`: every control on both the My Crate and Now Playing share sheets — close, section toggles, style chips, format segments, and save/share/copy. Imports `S`, `$`, `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`), `closeShareSheet`/`openPlayerShareSheet`/`closePlayerShareSheet`/`queueShareRender`/`queuePlayerShareRender`/`shareDoSave`/`shareDoShare`/`shareDoCopy`/`shareSelectedFormat` (`share/sheet.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `overlays.js` — `wireOverlays()`: three small single-button sheets that don't belong to any other area — the queue sheet (and its mini-player entry point), the look picker, and the library sort menu's close button. Imports `$`, `openQueueSheet`/`closeQueueSheet` (`player/queue.js`), `openLookPicker`/`closeLookPicker` (`record-art/looks.js`), `closeSortMenu` (`library/search.js`) — no `main.js` import.
  - `routes.js` — `wireRoutes()`: the one-line route tab bar — see the grouping rationale above. Imports `showRoute` (`shell/routes.js`) — no `main.js` import.
- `src/state.js` exports `S` (the one app-state object — audio context, tracks, queue, mode, character settings, sessions, etc.; `S.scrubbing` was a top-level `let` in `main.js` until step 3c, see rule 7), `current()` (`S.tracks[S.index] || null` — moved here in step 3b since nearly every part of the app calls it), plus `SOCIAL_SUPABASE_URL` and `SOCIAL_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`. The foundation every later extraction imports from. Since step 06 (B3), also the local state the backup-doc prerequisites called for back in step 03 (see "Backup: what gets synced" below for the full picture): `S.deviceId`/`S.deviceLabel` (this install's own random id/label, generated once and persisted — the `library_backup` row key), `S.historyClearedAt` (stamped by a full-scope "Clear listening history," `src/history/clear.js`), `S.sealedBroken` (a `Set` of album ids ever sealed-then-broken on this device — the durable signal `mergeSealed()`'s "broken anywhere stays broken" rule needs, since `S.sealed` alone can't tell "broken" apart from "never had this album"), `S.backupUpdatedAt` (one timestamp per per-field-last-write-wins key — `playlists`/`albumOrder`/`albumSort`/`albumDisplay`/`trackDisplay`/`albumEdge`/`albumLookPref`/`prefs`/`profileName`/**`picture`** (step 07)), and `S.backupRestoredFor` (guards the first-sign-in restore summary from firing twice for the same account). `touchBackupField(name)`, also exported here rather than from `storage/meta.js` (where it would otherwise belong next to `saveMeta`) specifically to avoid import-chain fan-out — every one of its call sites (`library/view.js`, `library/favorites.js`, `library/delete.js`, `library/thumbs.js`, `history/sessions.js`, `profile/screens.js`, `sync/backup.js` (step 07, for `picture`), plus `storage/meta.js` itself for `prefs`) already imports `S` from this same file, so routing it through here adds zero new edges — just stamps `S.backupUpdatedAt[name] = Date.now()`. Since step 07 (B4), `S.profile` also carries `pictureHash` (the re-encoded profile picture's own SHA-256, set by `sync/backup.js`'s `queueAvatarUpload`/`queueAvatarDownload` — `null` until a picture has ever been uploaded or downloaded on this device). Since step D2 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), also `S.theme` (`'auto'`/`'light'`/`'dark'`, default `'auto'` — saved via `storage/prefs.js` like any other setting, but device-only: `sync/backup.js`'s `buildLocalState()` strips it from the uploaded `prefs` object before it ever reaches the backup doc, per the "Backup: what gets synced" table below).
- `src/storage/idb.js` exports `DB` (step 4a) — the raw IndexedDB wrapper: `open`, `put`, `get`, `all`, `clear`, `del`, moved out of `main.js`'s "persistence" section unchanged. Database name `miziki`, version 4, the eight stores and their key paths, the non-destructive `onupgradeneeded`, the 4-second open timeout and the `onblocked` handling are all exactly as they were. `main.js` still calls `DB.put`/`DB.get`/etc. directly about 100 times, and `MizikiSocial.mizikiAdapter({S, DB, …})` still receives it directly too — both unchanged for now; replacing the ~100 direct calls with a named interface (and commenting the one adapter call site that's allowed to keep using `DB` directly) is step 4c. No `onversionchange` handler yet — a known gap, see "Persistence rules" below.
- `src/storage/prefs.js` exports two pure functions (step 4b): `serializePrefs(S)`, the exact object `saveMeta()` has always written under `meta`/`prefs`, and `parsePrefs(raw)`, which returns a clean object containing only the fields that pass the same checks `applyPrefs()` has always made inline (same type checks, same allowed values — see "Persistence rules" below) — anything that fails a check, or is simply missing, is left out. `applyPrefs()` takes `parsePrefs(raw)`'s output instead of the raw saved object; `saveMeta()` writes `serializePrefs(S)`. Splitting it this way makes the one part that matters for data safety — does an old or malformed saved object still load correctly — testable without a DOM.
- `src/storage/repo.js` (step 4c) — the named storage interface that replaced main.js's ~100 direct `DB.*` calls: one small object per store (`tracks`, `meta`, `sessions`, `achievements`, `collection`, `overlays`, `artwork`, `profile`), each with only the methods something in `main.js` actually calls, plus `storage.available()`/`storage.open()` replacing direct reads of `DB.ok` and the one `DB.open()` call. `meta.get(key)` returns the bare stored value instead of the `{k, v}` row every call site used to unwrap by hand; `meta.thumbs()` is the one place `main.js` scans all of `meta` rather than reading by key (artwork thumbnails), so it gets its own named method instead of a generic `all()`. `grep -n "DB\." src/main.js` finds only the two `MizikiSocial.mizikiAdapter({S, DB, …})` call sites (commented, since the adapter's own signature expects `DB`, not this interface) — no other direct `DB.*` call remains in `main.js`. A note at the bottom of the file (not built) records the App Store plan: blobs (`tracks.blob`, artwork blobs, `meta`'s `thumb:` blobs) would move to files on the device behind this same module, with callers unchanged.
- `src/storage/meta.js` (step 7a) — the "persistence" section: `queueSave` (exported; debounces `saveMeta` 600ms), the module-private `saveTimer`/`saveMeta`, `restoreMeta` (exported — the boot sequence's one caller) and `applyPrefs` (module-private, `restoreMeta`'s one caller). Since step 02 (F3 spec), `S.playlists[].items`/`S.albumOrder[album]` are themselves arrays of track ids (not positions — see `library/model.js`'s own entry), so `saveMeta`/`restoreMeta` write and read them straight through with no `idOf`/`idxOf` conversion at all any more; `restoreMeta` no longer drops an entry whose track is currently missing (that was the write-back bug step 5d flagged and this step fixed), and `library/model.js`'s `idOf`/`idxOf` imports were dropped from this file entirely as a result. `saveMeta`/`restoreMeta` read/write `serializePrefs`/`parsePrefs` (`storage/prefs.js`) for everything else, unchanged. `applyPrefs` keeps every one of its UI calls in the same order, now importing each from the module that owns it: `setMode` (`audio/mode.js`), `applyCharacter` (`audio/engine.js`), `applyVolume` (`sundown/location.js`), `toggleShuffle`/`cycleRepeat` (`player/controls.js`), `bgUpdateUI`/`applyBgAudioSetting` (`player/background.js`) — all real — plus `attachLaunchSettingRow` back from `main.js` (circular, temporary — see rule 8; the launch-intro section hasn't moved yet). Since step D2, `applyPrefs` also sets `S.theme` from a valid saved value and calls `applyTheme()` (`ui/theme.js`) unconditionally at the end — unconditionally so a restore with no saved `theme` at all (every device before D2) still re-applies the default (Auto) rather than leaving whatever `main.js`'s own boot-time call left in place stale. `ui/theme.js` imports `queueSave` back from here for `setTheme()`'s own save — a real circular pair, the same peer-import shape as `engine.js`/`transport.js`. `queueSave` is the one name six other modules used to reach circularly through `main.js` (`player/background.js`, `sundown/location.js`, `library/search.js`, `library/delete.js`, `library/favorites.js`, `library/import.js`) — all six now import it from here directly instead, each dropping (or shrinking) its own rule-8 entry. The stale "profile tab" / S5 / thumbnails / achievements / duplicate-scan leftover comments that used to sit in `main.js` just above the old persistence header were removed outright rather than moved again — each was pure documentation of code already relocated in steps 5c/5d/6b, and every destination module already carries its own copy of the relevant note.
- `src/shell/` (step 7b) — the navigation shell: the mini-player, the full-player overlay and its two ways in, the start/put-away ceremonies, and the gatefold-to-player hand-off. Together these are the most iOS-gesture-sensitive code in the app outside `player/transport.js` itself (rule 9) — every move below was checked against the pristine original lines with no `await` added, removed or reordered.
  - `mini-player.js` — "navigation shell: mini-player, full-player overlay, sleeve pull"'s own data and render step: `SLEEVE_ANIM` (the animation-timing constants, plus its `--sheet-duration`-style CSS custom-property side effects, run once at module load exactly as before), `renderMiniPlayer()`, `updateChromeInset()` (exported — the boot sequence's wiring calls it directly, and again on `resize`), `updateShellAlbum(t)`. `chromeInsetRAF`/`shellAlbumIdentity(t)` stay module-private. `GATEFOLD_ANIM` stayed in `main.js` on purpose — it's the gatefold's own open/close timing, physically interleaved with `SLEEVE_ANIM` in the original file but not mini-player/overlay code, and the gatefold itself hasn't moved yet (step 7e). Imports `S`/`current`, `$`, `albumKey`, `REDUCED` (`util/motion.js`), `bgSync` (`player/background.js`), `isLibraryRouteVisible` (`routes.js`, real), plus `applyCrateMiniInset`/`centerEmptyState` back from `main.js` (circular, temporary — see rule 8; both used only inside `updateChromeInset`'s existing `typeof === 'function'` guards, unchanged). Since step D3 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), also imports `isDesktopShell` (`util/viewport.js`) — `updateChromeInset()` treats the tab bar's own `offsetHeight` as `0` on desktop, since it's a full-viewport-height vertical sidebar there (not bottom chrome at all), not the real measurement `--chrome-inset` needs; without this guard the property would be inflated to roughly the sidebar's own height. `--chrome-inset` itself is moot on desktop regardless — `src/styles/23-desktop.css` overrides `.wrap`'s whole `padding` property there and never reads it — but a small, accurate fallback number beats a wildly wrong one sitting in a custom property nothing happens to consume today. Since step D4, `renderMiniPlayer()`'s own show/hide logic (unchanged) is what makes the strip real on desktop too — the D3-era `.mini-player{display:none !important}` placeholder is gone, replaced by `23-desktop.css`'s real 88px player-bar layout — so `updateChromeInset()`'s `miniH` measurement, and `applyCrateMiniInset()`'s own identical one (`crate/view.js`), now correctly pick up the bar's real height at desktop widths whenever a track is loaded, with no code change needed in either function for that to happen.
  - `sidebar.js` (step D3, `docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`) — the desktop sidebar's own new content: `renderSidebarPlaylists()`, the playlists list the mockup's sidebar shows and the phone layout has never needed. Built every time `renderTracks()` runs (`library/view.js`'s own existing "every `S.playlists` mutation already lands here" hub — see that module's entry) rather than from a second call site at each of `S.playlists`' own mutation points; hidden below the 1024px breakpoint entirely by CSS (`styles/23-desktop.css`), not a JS breakpoint check, so the DOM exists on every viewport, just invisible on a phone, the same way the tab bar's own buttons are shared between the tab bar and the sidebar through pure CSS reflow. Pins a system playlist (Favorites) first, the same order `library/view.js`'s own Playlists tab already uses. The sidebar's other new piece, the Sundown summary card, has no render function of its own here — see `player/clock.js`'s `drawSun()` entry for why. Imports `S`, `$`/`el` — no `main.js` import. Playlist names are user-typed, not a file's own embedded tag, but the same tag-injection rule (CLAUDE.md's "Security" section) applies regardless — built with `el()`/`textContent`, never `innerHTML`; `tests/unit/sidebar.test.js` feeds the doc's own required `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>` payload as a playlist name and confirms it survives as a single `textContent` string, never split or consumed as markup.
  - `routes.js` — the routing half of "navigation shell: header actions, search field, add menu": `isLibraryRouteVisible()`, `isProfileRouteVisible()`, `showRoute(name)`. Imports `$`, `renderProfile` (`profile/screens.js`, real), `renderHome` (`social/screens/home.js`, real), `updateHeaderActionsAvailability`/`closeSearchField`/`closeAddMenu` (`header.js`, a real circular pair — `header.js` imports `isLibraryRouteVisible`/`isProfileRouteVisible` back), plus `GF`/`closeGatefoldInstant` back from `main.js` (circular, temporary — see rule 8; `GF` is read-only here, per rule 7 — `showRoute` never reassigns it, only reads it).
  - `header.js` — the rest of "navigation shell: header actions, search field, add menu": `updateHeaderActionsAvailability()`, `updateLibTabsForLayout()`, `refreshLibraryTools()`, the header search field (`openSearchField`/`closeSearchField`/`isSearchFieldOpen`) and the header `+` menu (`openAddMenu`/`closeAddMenu`/`isAddMenuOpen`). Imports `S`, `$`, `formatBytes`/`totalLibraryBytes` (`library/delete.js`), `updateSearchChipsVisibility` (`library/search.js`), `isLibraryRouteVisible`/`isProfileRouteVisible` (`routes.js`, the real circular pair above), plus `libraryHasGenres` back from `main.js` (circular, temporary — see rule 8; the same `typeof === 'function'` guard it moved with).
  - `player-overlay.js` — `openPlayerViaSheet`, `openPlayer`, `closePlayer`, `morphInFlight`, `runMorphOpen`, `interruptMorph` — sat under `main.js`'s "launch intro" header only by physical accident (none of it is launch-intro code; flagged by the step 7 prompt itself as one of two stale headers to watch for). Imports `S`/`current`, `$`, `REDUCED` (`util/motion.js`), `play` (`player/transport.js`), `SLEEVE_ANIM` (`mini-player.js`, real), `runStartSequence`/`interruptStartSequence` (`start-sequence.js`, real, one-directional — `start-sequence.js` has no import back) — no `main.js` import at all.
  - `start-sequence.js` — "start sequence: sheet/pull/return orchestration": `waitAnimEnd(el, ms)`, `sleeveArtFor(art, hasArt)`, `runStartSequence(t, info, startAudio)`, `interruptStartSequence()`, plus the module-private `attachSleeveWrap`/`removeSleeveWrap`/`runSealPeel` (the sealed-record peel, SEALED spec §6). Imports `S`/`current`, `$`/`el`, `sleep`, `REDUCED` (`util/motion.js`), `albumKey`, `ensureBuffer` (`player/buffers.js`), `isAlbumSealed`/`buildSealOverlay`/`breakSeal` (`library/sealed.js`), `SLEEVE_ANIM`/`renderMiniPlayer` (`mini-player.js`, real), `play` (`player/transport.js`), `clearSpinDown` (`put-away.js`, a real circular pair — `put-away.js` imports `waitAnimEnd`/`sleeveArtFor` back, the same shape as `engine.js`/`transport.js`) — no `main.js` import at all.
  - `handoff.js` — "Part 2 §1: gatefold-to-player hand-off": `gfHandoffInFlight()`, `runGatefoldHandoff(startRect, t, info, startAudio)`, `interruptGatefoldHandoff()`, plus the module-private `HANDOFF_ANIM`/`handoffEaseInOut`. Imports `S`, `$`, `sleep`, `ensureBuffer` (`player/buffers.js`), `SLEEVE_ANIM` (`mini-player.js`, real — only `.holdCapMs`), `clearSpinDown` (`put-away.js`, real), `play` (`player/transport.js`), plus `isGatefoldOpen`/`closeGatefoldInstant` back from `main.js` (circular, temporary — see rule 8; the gatefold hasn't moved yet, step 7e).
  - `put-away.js` — "put-away: the record goes back in its sleeve": `shouldPutAway(auto)`, `clearSpinDown()`, `putAwayInstant()`, `runPutAwaySequence(t)`, plus the module-private `startSpinDown(ms)`. Imports `S`, `$`, `sleep`, `REDUCED` (`util/motion.js`), `SLEEVE_ANIM`/`renderMiniPlayer` (`mini-player.js`, real), `waitAnimEnd`/`sleeveArtFor` (`start-sequence.js`, the real circular pair above) — no `main.js` import at all. `easeOutCubic` moved to `util/motion.js` instead of staying here, despite sitting in the same original section — see that module's own entry for why.
  - `player-side-panel.js` (step D6, `docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`) — the desktop-only side panel beside the full player: `activePlayerSideTab()` (reads which of `#playerSideTabs`'s three buttons is currently `aria-selected="true"`, defaulting to `'upnext'`) and `renderPlayerSidePanel()` (repaints `#playerSideBody` with whichever of the three it is). None of the three renders are new work of their own — Up next is `player/queue.js`'s own `renderQueueSheet`, Liner notes and Credits are `library/credits.js`'s `renderLinerNotes`/`renderCreditsSheet` — this file is only the "which tab, repaint it" dispatcher the doc's own "the Up next... tabs call the same render functions the sheets use, targeted at the panel instead of an overlay" line calls for. Imports `current` (`state.js`), `$` (`util/dom.js`), `albumKey` (`record-art/tiers.js`), `renderQueueSheet` (`player/queue.js`), `renderCreditsSheet`/`renderLinerNotes` (`library/credits.js`) — no `main.js` import. Called from two places: `src/wiring/transport.js`'s three new tab-click listeners (plus one initial call at the end of `wireTransport()`), and `src/player/transport.js`'s `load()` (so the panel tracks whichever track just loaded, not just tab clicks) — see that file's own updated entry for the real, 3-node import cycle this creates with `player/queue.js`.
- `src/util/dom.js` exports `$()` (a `document.querySelector` shorthand) and `el()`, the tiny `createElement` helper used ~450 times across the app.
- `src/util/async.js` exports `sleep()`.
- `src/util/text.js` exports `normKey()`, the normalization helper (lowercase, NFKD, strip diacritics/punctuation) used ~40 times app-wide for matching artist/album/title.
- `src/util/math.js` exports `clamp()`.
- `src/util/motion.js` (step 7b) — two small motion-related primitives that used to be top-level `main.js` exports: `REDUCED` (the OS-level reduced-motion preference, read once at module load from `window.matchMedia`, unchanged) and `easeOutCubic()` (the easing curve the put-away sequence's spin-down uses, read by `player/clock.js`'s render loop). Both sat physically inside `main.js`'s "put-away" section, but neither is put-away-specific — `easeOutCubic` is only ever called from `clock.js`, not from `shell/put-away.js` itself. No imports, no `main.js` import.
- `src/util/viewport.js` (step D3, `docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`) — `DESKTOP_BREAKPOINT_PX` (`1024`, matching `docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`'s own desktop-shell breakpoint) and `isDesktopShell()` (a `matchMedia` check against it, called fresh each time rather than cached once at module load the way `util/motion.js`'s `REDUCED` is, since the viewport can change — e.g. resizing a desktop browser window across the breakpoint — in a way `prefers-reduced-motion` effectively never does mid-session). The one place the desktop/phone breakpoint is named in JavaScript; the breakpoint itself still has to be repeated once more, unavoidably, in the CSS media query (`src/styles/23-desktop.css`) a script can't read directly. No imports, no `main.js` import. Used by `shell/mini-player.js`'s `updateChromeInset()` and `crate/view.js`'s `applyCrateMiniInset()` — see each of their own entries for why.
- `src/record-art/tiers.js` — quality tiers (`trackTier`) and variant selection (`albumKey`, `trackIdentityKey`, `VARIANT_DEFS`, `selectVariant`, `variantBackground`) for record-art rendering.
- `src/record-art/metals.js` (step 6b) — the "metal tiers" section: `albumMetalFor`/`trackMetalFor` (threshold ladders in `ALBUM_METAL_THRESHOLDS`/`TRACK_METAL_THRESHOLDS`, exported for their unit test), `metalBackground`, `setDiscBackground`, `METAL_COLORS`. Imports only `S` and `$` — no DOM-dependent top level, no `main.js` import.
- `src/record-art/looks.js` (step 6b) — the "look switching" section: `resolveLook`, `applyDiscVariant` (the rare/metal/variant disc-background renderer — moved here rather than staying a separate export, since it's look-switching's own output, not a distinct concern), `openLookPicker`/`closeLookPicker` (exported; `availableLooks`/`setLookPref`/`lookLabel` stay module-private, used only by these). Imports `albumMetalFor`/`trackMetalFor`/`setDiscBackground` from `metals.js` and `persistAlbumLookPref` from `history/sessions.js` — a real circular import (looks.js ↔ sessions.js: sessions.js imports `applyDiscVariant` back), the same shape already established between `engine.js` and `transport.js`. No `main.js` import — this is what let `applyDiscVariant` become a *real* import everywhere it was previously a circular one back to `main.js` (`transport.js`, `library/edit.js`, `library/delete.js`, `history/sessions.js`/`played.js`/`clear.js`).
- `src/sundown/solar.js` — sun position math (`computeSun`, `solarEvent`, `nowClock`, `sunProgress`, `easedProgress`, `computeRate`) behind Sundown's auto playback rate.
- `src/sundown/location.js` — geolocation and Car Mode motion handling (`askLocation`, `fallbackSun`, `toggleSleevePull`, `toggleMotion`, `applyVolume`). Imports `drawSun` from `clock.js` and, since step 7a, `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` instead of circularly from `main.js` — a real circular pair with `meta.js` now (`meta.js` imports `applyVolume` back from here for `applyPrefs()`), the same shape as `engine.js`/`transport.js`.
- `src/crate/constants.js` — the small block of crate-view layout constants (`CRATE_ORIGIN_Y`, `CRATE_PALETTE`, `CRATE_VISIBLE_A`, `CRATE_DPR`, `CRATE_ALPHABET`).
- `src/crate/geometry.js` (step 7c) — `flowPose`/`flowZIndex`, the shared 3D-pose math (CRATE spec §3: "one geometry implementation so the two views can never drift apart") used by both the launch overlay's card stack and the crate shelf itself. Pure, zero imports — moved verbatim out of `main.js`'s old launch-intro section.
- `src/crate/tiers.js` (step 7c) — the "S4: crate image tiers" section: `crateArtCache` (the `{S, L}` LRU cache, exported read-only for `launch/intro.js`'s own card-art lookup), `buildCrateTier`, `getCrateArtURL`, `deleteCrateArtTiers`, `healCrateArt`, `prewarmCrateAnchorL`. Imports `S`, `sleep` (`util/async.js`), `albumKey` (`record-art/tiers.js`), `artwork` (`storage/repo.js`) — no `main.js` import.
- `src/crate/model.js` (step 7c) — the crate data model, split out of the same old "S4" section it had been sitting in only by physical accident (grouping/sorting albums for the shelf isn't image-tier work): `libraryHasGenres`, `ensureCrateModel`, `invalidateCrateModel`, `isNowPlayingAlbum`, `persistCrateAnchor`, `crateRailJumpTarget`. Imports `S`, `normKey` (`util/text.js`), `albumKey` (`record-art/tiers.js`), `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`), `CRATE_ALPHABET` (`./constants.js`) — no `main.js` import.
- `src/crate/view.js` (step 7c) — the actual crate-shelf rendering (`applyCrateMiniInset`, `renderCrate`, `centerEmptyState`), which had been sitting inside `main.js`'s misleadingly-named "S3: gatefold album view" header purely by physical accident — that header also covers the gatefold itself, the sealed-record sheet and `renderTracks`, none of which moved here. Imports `S`, `$`/`el`, `clamp`, `REDUCED`, the crate-view constants from `./constants.js`, `flowPose`/`flowZIndex` from `./geometry.js`, `isAlbumSealed`/`buildSealOverlay` from `library/sealed.js`, `thumbURL` from `library/thumbs.js`, `setQueue` from `player/queue.js`, `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js`, `isLibraryRouteVisible` from `shell/routes.js`, `getCrateArtURL`/`prewarmCrateAnchorL` from `./tiers.js`, `ensureCrateModel`/`invalidateCrateModel`/`isNowPlayingAlbum`/`persistCrateAnchor`/`crateRailJumpTarget`/`libraryHasGenres` from `./model.js`, and `resolveLastPlayedAlbum` from `../launch/intro.js` (real, one-directional — `intro.js` doesn't import back, so this isn't a circular pair). Since step 7d, `renderTracks` is a real import from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — a real circular pair with it (`view.js` imports `centerEmptyState`/`renderCrate` back from here), the same peer-import shape as `engine.js`/`transport.js`. Plus a temporary circular import back to `main.js` for `openGatefold`/`openSealedSheet`/`sealedQuietPlay` (rule 8 — the gatefold and sealed-sheet code is 7e's territory). Since step D3, also imports `isDesktopShell` (`util/viewport.js`) — `applyCrateMiniInset()`'s own phone-layout height budget (`window.innerHeight` minus the fixed tab bar's and mini-player's heights) treats the tab bar's `getBoundingClientRect().height` as `0` on desktop, the same reasoning and the same fix as `shell/mini-player.js`'s `updateChromeInset()` right above it — the tab bar is a full-height vertical sidebar there, not bottom chrome, so its height has nothing to do with this budget. Since step D5 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), this module also carries the crate's own desktop layout — a larger card, scaled stage geometry, and the inline record-detail panel that replaces the gatefold as the way in on a tap, at desktop widths only:
  - `currentCardSize()` (module-private) reads `--crate-card-size`'s live computed value (`10-crate.css`'s own `278px` default, overridden to `340px` at `1024px+` by `23-desktop.css`) rather than hardcoding either number, falling back to the 278px baseline if the property somehow doesn't resolve. `renderCrate(host)` computes `cardSize`/`cardSpread` (`cardSize / 278`) once per render and threads both through: `buildSealOverlay(cardSize)` in place of the old literal `278`, and `flowPose(a, d, speed, {originY: CRATE_ORIGIN_Y, spread: cardSpread})` in `syncCards` — reusing `flowPose()`'s own pre-existing `opts.spread` multiplier (previously only exercised by the launch intro's animated flourish, `launch/intro.js`) to scale the vertical offset between stacked cards proportionally to the card's own new size, so a bigger desktop card never overlaps its neighbours any more tightly than the 278px phone baseline already does — zero changes to `flowPose()` itself (`crate/geometry.js`).
  - `openCrateAlbum(item)` gained one new branch, between the existing sealed check and the `openGatefold(item)` call: `if(isDesktopShell()){ renderCrateDetailPanel(item); return; }`. A sealed record is unaffected either way — it still opens the sealed sheet on both phone and desktop, exactly as before this step. The gatefold itself is untouched and stays the phone's own way in.
  - `renderCrateDetailPanel(item)`/`renderCrateDetailPlaceholder(panel)` (both new, module-private) build the desktop-only inline panel's content into `#crateDetailPanel`: cover art (reusing `thumbURL`/the track's own embedded `art`, the same fallback order the crate card face already uses), a "Play album" button (`setQueue(item.idx, 0, true)`), and the tracklist — built by calling the gatefold's own, already-exported `buildGatefoldTracklist(item, trackIdx, playHandler)`/`gatefoldTrackIndices(item)` (`gatefold/view.js`) directly, rather than reimplementing tracklist rendering a second time. This is a real, one-directional import addition (`gatefold/view.js` doesn't import anything back for this), riding alongside the module's existing circular import of `openGatefold` from the same file. The panel is deliberately scoped to just these three things for this first cut — no credits/liner-notes/social controls, matching the doc's own "New" column, which names only the inline detail panel, the larger stage, and the tab-row toggle, not the gatefold's fuller feature set. `renderCrateDetailPlaceholder()` is what the panel shows before anything's been tapped ("Tap a record to see its track list.").
  - `renderCrate(host)` wraps the existing `.crate-wrap` (unchanged — same `nowPlayingBtn`/`stage`/`rail`/`info` children, same `applyCrateMiniInset()` contract, since it's still `position:relative` and still found by the exact same `document.querySelector('.crate-wrap')`-style lookups) and a new sibling `#crateDetailPanel` (`.crate-detail-panel`) inside a new `.crate-layout` wrapper — the same "invisible scaffolding below the breakpoint via `display:contents`" pattern `.sidebar` established in D3, so `.crate-wrap`'s own phone layout, DOM position, and `applyCrateMiniInset()`'s own math are all completely unaffected below 1024px.
  - `updateCrateGroupToggleUI()` gained a tab-row mirror: alongside the existing Profile → Library tools copy (`#crateGroupToggle`), a new desktop-only copy (`#crateGroupToggleTab`, new ids, in the Library route's own tab row — "Crate order toggle in the tab row," the doc's third "New" item) is shown/hidden by `isDesktopShell()` and kept in sync with `S.crateGroup`/`hasGenres` every time this function runs (i.e., on every crate re-render) — a mirror of existing state, not a relocation or a clone of the existing control, the same "new ids driving the same state" shape D3's sidebar mirrors and D4's player-bar controls both already established. A bare resize across the breakpoint while already on the crate screen doesn't immediately re-sync this (nothing currently ties it to the existing `resize` listener in `main.js`) — a small, accepted gap, the same class as several other `isDesktopShell()`-is-read-fresh-not-resize-synced spots already in this codebase; the next crate re-render self-corrects.
- `src/launch/intro.js` (step 7c) — the launch intro sequence, moved out of `main.js`'s old "launch intro" section (which also held the player overlay/morph and shared crate geometry — neither moved here, see those modules' own entries): `resolveLastPlayedAlbum`, `resolveLastPlayedTrackIndex`, `sampleStackAlbums`/`computeFlickPosition`/`flickSpeed` (each given `export`, previously private, for their own unit tests — same precedent as `angleDelta` in `scrub.js`, step 3c), `runLaunchSequence`, `attachLaunchSettingRow`, `toggleLaunchIntro`, and `markLibraryReady()` — a small wrapper added around the module-private `libraryReadyResolve`/`libraryReadyPromise` pair (rule 7: an importing module can't reassign an exported `let`, so the boot sequence's two `libraryReadyResolve()` call sites in `main.js` now call this instead). Imports `S`, `$`, `sleep`, `clamp`, `albumKey`/`trackIdentityKey` (`record-art/tiers.js`), `albumTracks`/`groupBy` (`library/model.js`), `thumbURL` (`library/thumbs.js`), `queueSave` (`storage/meta.js`), `flowPose`/`flowZIndex` (`../crate/geometry.js`), `crateArtCache` (`../crate/tiers.js`) — no `main.js` import. `toggleScratch`, which had been sitting in this section only because that's where the Character menu's Scratch toggle wiring happened to land, moved into `src/player/scrub.js` instead (that module already owns Scratch — see its own entry).
- `src/player/sleep-timer.js` — the sleep timer (SLEEP spec §5): `updateSleepUI`, `stopSleepState`, `sleepStopPlayback`, `sleepCheckDeadline`, `openSleepSheet`, `closeSleepSheet`. Imports `pause` from `transport.js` and `drawTime` from `clock.js`. No longer imports anything from `main.js` (step 3c).
- `src/audio/engine.js` — the audio graph (`makeContext`, `buildGraph`, `satCurve`, `applyCharacter`, `routeSource`) and `ensureContext()` (from the "file loading" section — rebuilds the context when a file's sample rate differs). Imports `applyVolume` from `location.js`, `stop` from `transport.js`, and, since step 6c, `applyOutputRoute` from `player/background.js` (a real circular import — background.js imports `ensureContext` back from here for its Media Session "play" handler, the same shape already established between `engine.js` and `transport.js`). No `main.js` import at all, since step 6c.
- `src/player/transport.js` — `load`, `startSource`, `play`, `stop`, `pause`, `seek` (step 3b). The iOS-gesture-critical path: `bgPrime()` is the first call in `play()`, before any `await`, because iOS only allows audio to start inside the original tap; no `await` was added, removed or reordered when this moved, and the `S.gen` generation counter / `onended` logic (how a stale source is kept from advancing the queue) and the encoder delay/padding handling in `startSource` (PLAYBACK spec §1) are unchanged. Imports `drawTime`/`settleTrackEnd` from `clock.js`, `setPathNote` from `ui/path-note.js`, `advance`/`preloadNextTrack` from `player/queue.js`, `ensureBuffer` from `player/buffers.js`, `sessionOnLoad`/`touchActiveSession` from `history/sessions.js`, `updateCreditsButtonForCurrent`/`updateFavoriteButtons` from `library/credits.js`/`library/favorites.js` (all real imports as of step 5a/5c/5e, previously circular through `main.js`), and, since step 6c, `bgPrime`/`bgRouteActive`/`bgIdleNow`/`bgPosition` from `player/background.js` (a real circular import — background.js imports `play`/`pause`/`seek` back from here for its Media Session action handlers, same shape as above). Since step 7d, `renderTracks` is a real import from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — only `updateGatefoldNowPlaying` (gatefold, 7e) remains a circular import back to `main.js`. See rule 8 and the step 3b/3c/5a/5c/5e/6c/7d changelog entries below for the full list and the future-cleanup note. Since step D6 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), `load()` also calls `renderPlayerSidePanel` (`shell/player-side-panel.js`) at its own end, the same single choke point `updateCreditsButtonForCurrent()`/`drawTime()` already use for "something that must track `current()`" — keeps the desktop side panel's active tab in step with whichever track just loaded. `S.queue`/`S.qpos` are always updated by `load()`'s own caller (`setQueue()`/`advance()`, both in `player/queue.js`) before `load()` itself runs, so the panel's own "Up next" branch never sees stale queue state.
- `src/player/clock.js` — the render loop (step 3c): `tick`, `settleTrackEnd`, `frame`, the hidden-page `setInterval` fallback, `fmt`, `drawTime`, `drawSun`. Imports `sleepCheckDeadline` from `sleep-timer.js`, `computeRate`/`sunProgress`/`easedProgress` from `solar.js`, `addPlayedRange`/`checkTrackCompletion` from `history/played.js` (real since step 5c), and, since step 6c, `bgPosition` from `player/background.js` (real, not circular — clock.js calls it, background.js never calls back into clock.js), plus `easeOutCubic`/`REDUCED` back from `main.js` (circular, same reason as above). The `maxDt` caps (0.1s for frames, 120s for the hidden-page timer), the order of work in `tick` (sleep deadline, then rate, then position, then played ranges and completion) and the 90ms/220ms redraw throttles are all unchanged — sessions, metals and the 95% rule depend on this clock. Since step D3 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), `drawSun()` also mirrors its own `#rateWhy`/`#legStart`/`#legEnd` readout into the desktop sidebar's own `#sideRateWhy`/`#sideLegStart`/`#sideLegEnd` ids (null-guarded — those ids exist only in the sidebar's own DOM, `src/shell/sidebar.js`, hidden below the breakpoint by CSS, so the guard is "does the sidebar card exist," never a breakpoint check) — per that doc's own instruction to reuse this exact readout rather than a second module calling `sunProgress()`/`computeRate()` on its own. Since step D4, `drawTime()` gained the same kind of guarded mirror, into the desktop player bar's own `#barElapsed`/`#barTotal`/`#barScrub` — `#tElapsed`/`#tTotal`/`#scrub` stay the full player's own ids (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`'s own "duplicate element ids are not allowed" rule), so the bar's seek readout is a second small mirror of the same already-computed values, not a second position computation.
- `src/player/background.js` (step 6c) — "background playback (optional)": `bgPrime`, `bgRouteActive`, `bgIdleNow`, `bgPosition`, `bgSync`, `bgUpdateUI`, `toggleBgAudio`, `applyBgAudioSetting`, `applyOutputRoute`, and the Media Session / lock-screen handlers (`mediaSessionSync`, `bgInstallHandlers`, `bgRemoveHandlers`, kept module-private). `bgPrime()` is still the very first call in `play()` (`player/transport.js`), before any `await` — moving where it's *defined* changed nothing about when it *runs* (rule 9). The lock-screen `previoustrack`/`nexttrack` handlers still skip tracks rather than seek (PR #42's behavior, untouched), and every Media Session action handler and position-state update installs/fires in the same order as before. Imports `ensureContext` from `audio/engine.js` and `play`/`pause`/`seek` from `player/transport.js` (both real circular imports, see those modules' own entries above), `prevTrack`/`nextTrack` from `player/controls.js` (real, for the lock-screen handlers), `setPathNote` from `ui/path-note.js`, and, since step 7a, `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` instead of circularly from `main.js` — a real circular pair with `meta.js` now (`meta.js` imports `bgUpdateUI`/`applyBgAudioSetting` back from here for `applyPrefs()`) — for `toggleBgAudio`'s own save.
- `src/player/controls.js` (step 6c) — `nextTrack`, `prevTrack`, `toggleShuffle`, `cycleRepeat` (previously sitting under `main.js`'s "persistence" header, where they'd landed only because that's where this part of "visible queue" happened to be — none of them touch persistence). Imports `seek` from `player/transport.js`, `advance`/`reorderQueue` from `player/queue.js`, `invalidateActiveSessionIfAny` from `history/sessions.js` (all real) — no `main.js` import at all.
- `src/audio/mode.js` (step 6c) — `setMode` (previously sitting under `main.js`'s "share image" header, for the same reason as above — it's mode switching, not sharing). Imports `routeSource` from `audio/engine.js` (real) — no `main.js` import at all. F1's Pure-rate rule (`computeRate()` in `sundown/solar.js` returning exactly `1` in Pure) lives entirely in `solar.js` and was untouched by this move — `setMode` only sets `S.mode` and re-routes the current source; `computeRate()` reads `S.mode` the same way it always did. Since step D4, `setMode()` also mirrors the same `aria-pressed` state it sets on `#modePure`/`#modeVinyl` onto the desktop player bar's own `#barModePure`/`#barModeVinyl` — new ids, not clones of the full player's own, per `docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`'s "duplicate element ids are not allowed" rule.
- `src/player/scrub.js` — spin-to-scrub (step 3c): `wireSpinToScrub`, `angleDelta` (exported for its unit test, same as `solarEvent` in step 2c), and their constants, plus `toggleScratch` (step 7c — the Character menu's Scratch toggle handler, moved here from `main.js`'s old launch-intro section, where it had been sitting only by physical accident; this module already owns everything else about Scratch). Imports `drawTime` from `clock.js`, `pause`/`play`/`seek` from `transport.js`, and `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` (for `toggleScratch`'s own save) — no `main.js` import. Since F2, this is also where true scratching lives: when a gesture is claimed in Vinyl with `S.char.scratch` on, it lazily loads `src/audio/scratch-processor.js` into an `AudioWorkletNode`, copies a ±10s window of the current track's decoded channel data to it, and streams `target`/`window` messages to it on every `pointermove` (see `SCRATCH_SEC_PER_ROTATION`, `SCRATCH_WINDOW_SEC`). Scratch off, or Pure mode, falls back to the original silent fast-scrub unchanged.
- `src/audio/scratch-processor.js` — the F2 `AudioWorkletProcessor` scrub.js loads to produce real scratch audio. A plain classic script — no `import`, no `export` — both because Vite copies it verbatim via `new URL(..., import.meta.url)` in `scrub.js` rather than bundling it, and because Safari's AudioWorklet module loader has not reliably supported ES module syntax in the processor file itself (an early version that used `export` worked on desktop but silently produced no scratch audio on an iPhone). `tests/unit/scratch-processor.test.js` loads its source with Node's `vm` module to reach its pure math (`clampIndex`, `sampleAt`, `onePole`, `velocityEnvelopeTarget`) instead of importing it.
- `src/ui/path-note.js` — `setPathNote()` (step 3c), the general on-screen note helper. It happened to live in the spin-to-scrub section but isn't about scrubbing, so it got its own module rather than moving into `scrub.js`. No `main.js` import.
- `src/ui/theme.js` (step D2, `docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`) — the Auto/Light/Dark appearance preference: `applyTheme()` (sets/removes `document.documentElement.dataset.theme` per `S.theme`, mirrors a pinned choice to `localStorage` for `src/theme-boot.js`'s own anti-flash read, updates `<meta name="theme-color">` since a meta attribute can't read a CSS custom property, installs/tears down a `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)')` listener so Auto keeps following the device live — e.g. at sunset — and syncs `aria-pressed` on every `[data-theme-choice]` button currently in the DOM, phone or desktop) and `setTheme(choice)` (validates, sets `S.theme`, persists via `queueSave()`, applies — the one function both the phone Character-menu row and the desktop sidebar's segmented control, D7, call). Imports `S` from `../state.js` and `queueSave` from `../storage/meta.js` — a real circular pair with `meta.js` (which imports `applyTheme` back, for `applyPrefs()`'s own theme handling), the same peer-import shape as `engine.js`/`transport.js`. No `main.js` import — `main.js` itself imports `applyTheme` directly (one-directional) to call it once, unconditionally, in the boot sequence, the same shape `setMode('vinyl')` already has there.
- `src/theme-boot.js` (step D2) — the anti-flash head script: a plain classic script (no import/export, like `src/miziki-social.js`), loaded early in `<head>`, before the stylesheet `<link>` tags, so it blocks parsing and sets `document.documentElement.dataset.theme` from the `localStorage` mirror `src/ui/theme.js` keeps in sync — before any CSS is even applied — for a *pinned* Light or Dark choice. Auto needs none of this (no JS at first paint): with no `data-theme` attribute set, `src/styles/22-theme.css`'s own `@media (prefers-color-scheme)` block handles it on its own. The `copy-theme-boot` Vite plugin (`vite.config.js`) keeps it at this same `src/` path under the build output, the same way `copy-social-client` already does for `miziki-social.js`.
- `src/library/model.js` (step 5a) — pure library-indexing helpers, moved out of main.js's "library", "favorites" and "persistence" sections: `byName`/`trackSort` (sort comparators), `groupBy`, `allIdx`, `albumTracks` (per-album auto/title/manual ordering), `idOf`/`idxOf` (track id ↔ index lookups, used by every saved order/playlist). No DOM, no `main.js` import — reads only `S.tracks` and the small per-album prefs on `S`. Since step 02 (F3 spec), `albumTracks`'s manual mode keeps `S.albumOrder[album]` as an array of ids, not positions — an id resolves via `idxOf` and is filtered against the album's current membership for display, but a missing or out-of-album id is never dropped from the saved array itself, so a track's manual position survives being forgotten (`forgetLibrary()`) and reconnects once the same file is re-added. See `src/library/favorites.js`/`src/library/view.js`/`src/library/delete.js`/`src/storage/meta.js`'s own entries for the same change applied to playlists.
- `src/player/queue.js` (step 5a) — the "queue, shuffle, repeat" section plus the "visible queue" section's `advance()`/`preloadNextTrack()`: `buildOrder`, `setQueue`, `reorderQueue`, `preloadNextTrack`, `advance`. The rest of "visible queue" — `queueInsert`/`playNext`/`addToQueue`, `queueRemoveAt`, `queueMove`, and the queue-sheet UI (`renderQueueSheet`/`openQueueSheet`/`closeQueueSheet`) — moved in here too, in step 5e (`nextTrack`/`prevTrack`/`toggleShuffle`/`cycleRepeat`, which happened to sit in the same section, stayed in `main.js` — none of them are visible-queue manipulation). Imports `load`/`play`/`pause`/`seek` from `transport.js` (a real circular import — transport.js imports `advance`/`preloadNextTrack` back from here, the same shape already established between `engine.js` and `transport.js`), `drawTime` from `clock.js`, `sleepStopPlayback` from `sleep-timer.js`, `albumKey` from `tiers.js`, `ensureBuffer` from `buffers.js`, `invalidateActiveSessionIfAny` from `history/sessions.js` (real as of step 5c, previously circular), and five screen functions back from `main.js` (circular, temporary — see rule 8). Since step D6 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`), `renderQueueSheet` is exported and takes an optional `host` (defaulting to `#queueBody`, the phone overlay's own element, unchanged for that call site) — the desktop side panel's "Up next" tab (`shell/player-side-panel.js`) targets it at `#playerSideBody` instead, same render, different host. A new module-private `queueSheetHost` remembers whichever host was last painted into, so `queueMove`/`queueRemoveAt` (a move/remove click made from *inside* whichever one is actually visible) re-render that same one rather than always defaulting back to `#queueBody`. `shell/player-side-panel.js` picks up a real, one-directional import of `renderQueueSheet` from here (`queue.js` imports nothing back from it, so not a circular pair on its own) — but since `player-side-panel.js` is in turn imported by `player/transport.js`'s `load()`, and `queue.js` already imports `load`/`play`/`pause`/`seek` from `transport.js`, the three files together form a real 3-node cycle (`transport.js` → `player-side-panel.js` → `queue.js` → `transport.js`) — unremarkable by this codebase's own standards (`library/view.js` alone has seven such pairs), noted here since it's new as of this step.
- `src/player/buffers.js` (step 5a) — the decoded-audio LRU cache pulled out of the old "clear listening history" section: `touchLRU`, `ensureBuffer`. Imports `tracks` from `storage/repo.js`, `setPathNote` from `ui/path-note.js`, `ensureContext` from `audio/engine.js`. No `main.js` import.
- `src/library/tags/` (step 5b) — the byte-level format parsers that used to be `main.js`'s whole "file loading" section, split by format: `bytes.js` (shared low-level byte/text primitives: `txt`, `u32be`, `synch`, `artURL`, `decodeText`, `clean`), `details.js` (the shared liner-notes/credits helpers every format's `*Details()` uses: `emptyDetails`, `splitNames`, `parsePerformerEntry`, `parseYear`, `creditRoleBucket`), `flac.js`, `mp4.js` (MP4/ALAC/AAC), `mp3.js` (the raw MPEG header `mp3Info()` scans for — ID3 tag/details/artwork reading is in `id3.js`, since ID3 is also how MP3 and AIFF files carry tags), `id3.js`, `wav.js`, and `index.js` — the public surface (`readHeader`, `readTags`, `readDetails`, `findArtworkBlob`, plus `emptyDetails` re-exported for convenience) that dispatches to the right format module by sniffing the file's magic bytes. Pure functions of bytes throughout — no DOM, no `S`, no `main.js` import. None of the per-format helpers (`flacTags`, `mp4Cover`, `id3Details`, etc.) are called from outside this directory; only the five names `index.js` exports are.
- `src/library/import.js` (step 5b) — `addFiles()`'s import loop (and its drag-and-drop file-walking helpers `filesFromDataTransferItems`/`walkEntry`/`readDirEntries`), `addTestTone`, `storeTrack` (moved here from the "metadata editing" section, where it sat only because that's where step 4c's call-site migration found it — see its own changelog entry), and `findDuplicateTrack`/`DUP_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SEC`. "What to do with a File object," not "how to read one" — the byte-level parsing is `library/tags/`'s job. Imports `readHeader`/`readTags`/`readDetails`/`findArtworkBlob`/`emptyDetails` from `tags/index.js`, `askDuplicateResolution` from `duplicates-ui.js`, `albumKey`/`trackIdentityKey` from `tiers.js`, `setPathNote`, `ensureContext`, `touchLRU`, `setQueue`/`allIdx`, `storage`/`tracks` from their respective modules, `loadOverlayFor` from `edit.js`, `deleteTracks` from `delete.js`, `rebuildPeopleIndex` from `credits.js` (all real imports as of step 5b/5d/5e), `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` instead of circularly from `main.js` (step 7a), and, since step 7d, `renderTracks` from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — no `main.js` import at all, since step 7d. The import loop's behavior is unchanged: one file at a time, the "Importing N of M" note, a failed file skipped (not aborting the batch), new albums sealed once per batch (SEALED spec §2).
- `src/library/duplicates-ui.js` (step 5b) — the single-file duplicate prompt shown during import (`askDuplicateResolution`, `closeDupOverlay`) plus `closeDuplicateScan` (closes the separate library-wide duplicate-scan overlay opened by `openDuplicateScan()`, see `duplicate-scan.js` below — kept here rather than moved there since it doesn't touch `findAllDuplicateGroups`/`renderDupScan`, just the dialog plumbing shared with `askDuplicateResolution()`). Only imports `$` from `util/dom.js` — no `S`, no `main.js` import.
- `src/library/edit.js` (step 5d) — the "metadata editing: sidecar overlay" section: `loadOverlayFor`, `cropSquareImage`, `applyEdit`, `revertTrack`, `applyEditWithUndo`, `undoLastEdit`, the undo banner, and the edit sheet (`openEditSheet`/`closeEditSheet`/`saveEditSheet`). Imports `idxOf` from `model.js`, `albumKey` from `tiers.js`, `overlays`/`artwork`/`storage` from `repo.js`, `applyDiscVariant` from `record-art/looks.js`, `persistSealedAlbums` from `sealed.js`, `renderMiniPlayer` from `shell/mini-player.js`, `deleteCrateArtTiers`/`buildCrateTier` from `crate/tiers.js` (all real), and, since step 7d, `renderTracks`/`exitSelectMode` from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — a real circular pair with it (`view.js` imports `applyEdit`/`showUndoBanner` back from here). Since step 07 (B4), also imports `scheduleBackupSync`/`queueCoverUpload` from `sync/backup.js` (real, one-directional — `backup.js` doesn't import anything back from here): `applyEdit()` calls `queueCoverUpload(id, artBlob)` fire-and-forget whenever a custom cover is set, and `revertTrack()`/`undoLastEdit()`'s "no prior overlay" tombstone branch now writes `cover: null` alongside the existing `fields: null, reverted: true` — a revert clears the backed-up cover claim the same way it clears the text fields, both as one tombstone write.
- `src/library/delete.js` (step 5d) — the "deletion" section: `countForAlbum`/`countForArtist`, `formatBytes`/`totalLibraryBytes`, `deleteTracks`, `deleteAlbum`/`deleteArtist`, `forgetLibrary`. `deleteTracks()` removes files from Miziki's library only, never from disk; listening history is untouched by design (LIBRARY spec §1 — see the module's own header comment) — since step 02, dropping a deleted id from `S.playlists`/`S.albumOrder` is a plain `.filter()` (items are ids, not positions, see `library/model.js`'s own entry), replacing the old snapshot-to-ids-then-rebuild-to-indices dance the index-based representation used to need. **`forgetLibrary()`'s own meaning changed in step 02 (F3 spec): it now removes only the `tracks` store, this album's crate-art tiers, and thumbnails nothing else still references — `meta`/`sessions`/`overlays`/`achievements`/`collection` are never cleared**, so play counts, sessions, metal/rare progress, playlists (including Favorites), track edits, custom covers, manual order/sort and sealed state all wait and reconnect automatically once a file with the same id (name|size|lastModified) is re-added; "Clear listening history" (`history/clear.js`) stays the only path that resets stats. Imports `idOf`/`idxOf`/`allIdx` from `model.js`, `tracks`/`overlays`/`artwork` from `repo.js` (step 02 dropped the now-unused `meta`/`sessions` imports, since `forgetLibrary()` no longer touches either store directly), `stop` from `transport.js`, `setQueue` from `queue.js`, `stopSleepState` from `sleep-timer.js`, `drawTime` from `clock.js`, `invalidateActiveSessionIfAny` from `history/sessions.js`, `rebuildPeopleIndex` from `credits.js`, `applyDiscVariant` from `record-art/looks.js`, `persistSealedAlbums` from `library/sealed.js`, `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` instead of circularly from `main.js` (step 7a), `clearSpinDown` from `shell/put-away.js`, `closePlayer` from `shell/player-overlay.js`, `renderMiniPlayer` from `shell/mini-player.js`, `deleteCrateArtTiers` from `crate/tiers.js`, `invalidateCrateModel` from `crate/model.js`, `renderTracks` from `library/view.js` (since step 7d — a real circular pair with it, `view.js` imports `countForAlbum`/`countForArtist`/`deleteAlbum`/`deleteArtist` back from here), and, since step 02, `pruneThumbs` from `library/thumbs.js` (real, one-directional — `thumbs.js` doesn't import anything back from `delete.js`, so not a circular pair) for `forgetLibrary()`'s own "drop thumbnails nothing else needs" step.
- `src/library/duplicate-scan.js` (step 5d) — the "duplicate scan (library-wide)" section: `findAllDuplicateGroups`, `openDuplicateScan`, `renderDupScan` — the "scan my whole library" screen reached from settings, separate from the single-file prompt shown during import. Imports `trackIdentityKey` from `tiers.js` and `deleteTracks` from the real `delete.js` — no `main.js` import.
- `src/library/credits.js` (step 5e) — combines two adjacent sections: "liner notes & credits" (CREDITS spec §1 — `trackHasCreditsContent`, `splitComments`/`commentQualifies`, the credits sheet `renderCreditsSheet`/`openCreditsSheet`/`closeCreditsSheet`, `updateCreditsButtonForCurrent`, `albumLinerNotes`, `albumCreditRoll`) and "credits that link: person view" (CREDITS spec §2 — `openPersonOrArtist`, `creditRow`/`creditNamesCell`/`creditLinkButton`, the lazily-rebuilt `peopleIndex` Map and `rebuildPeopleIndex`, `renderPersonView`). `peopleIndex` itself stays module-private — `personEntryFor(name)` is a new named accessor added for `search.js`'s one lookup (the "People" result group), the same shape as `storage/repo.js`'s `meta.get`/`meta.thumbs()`. Imports `byName`/`albumTracks` from `model.js`, `emptyDetails` from `tags/index.js`, `setQueue` from `player/queue.js`, `closePlayer` from `shell/player-overlay.js`, `showRoute` from `shell/routes.js` (all real), and, since step 7d, `renderTracks`/`songRow` from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — a real circular pair with it (`view.js` imports `trackHasCreditsContent`/`albumLinerNotes`/`albumCreditRoll`/`creditRow`/`renderPersonView` back from here). Since step D6 (`docs/DESKTOP_LAYOUT.md`): `renderCreditsSheet` is exported and takes an optional `host` (defaulting to `#creditsBody`); the `$('#creditsTitle').textContent = t.tags.title` line that used to open the function moved up into `openCreditsSheet` instead, since it's overlay-sheet chrome, not part of "build credits content for this track" — `renderCreditsSheet` itself now also guards a falsy `t` (never reachable from the existing `#trackInfoBtn` call site, but reachable from the desktop side panel before anything has ever played) with a plain "Nothing is playing" fallback instead of throwing. A new `renderLinerNotes(host, album)` — the one genuinely new render function this step adds, since `albumLinerNotes()` itself was always a pure string accessor with no render counterpart of its own on the phone — paints that string (or a "No liner notes in these files." fallback, the exact copy the gatefold's own left-panel minimal-note already uses) into a host. Both are called by the new `shell/player-side-panel.js`'s "Credits"/"Liner notes" tabs, targeted at `#playerSideBody` instead of either the phone's own `#creditsBody` or anything gatefold-specific.
- `src/library/favorites.js` (step 5e) — "favorites: a real playlist, not a separate flag" (CREDITS/FAVORITES spec §4): `isFavorite`, `toggleFavorite`, `updateFavoriteButtons`. Favorites are a system playlist (`S.playlists`, `system:'favorites'`) — the `system:'favorites'` tag has to survive every place playlists are copied (`saveMeta`/`restoreMeta`, `deleteTracks`' id filter) or it's lost on reload. Since step 02 (F3 spec), playlist items are track ids, not positions (see `library/model.js`'s own entry) — `isFavorite`/`toggleFavorite` take a track id and read/write `pl.items` directly with no index lookup at all any more, so the module's `idxOf` import from `model.js` is gone; favoriting a track id that isn't currently in the library (forgotten, not deleted) still records it, same as any other playlist entry. Imports `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` instead of circularly from `main.js` (step 7a), `REDUCED` from `util/motion.js` (step 7b), and, since step 7d, `renderTracks` from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — a real circular pair with it (`view.js` imports `isFavorite` back from here).
- `src/library/search.js` (step 5e) — combines "library-wide search + sort" (`librarySortBar`/`openSortMenu`/`closeSortMenu`, `librarySortComparator`, `sortAlbumGroups`) and "tag-aware search" (CREDITS spec §3 — `parseSearchQuery`, `trackMatchesField` and its field-specific helpers, `searchLibrary`, `highlightMatches`/`highlightRowLabel`, `renderSearchResults`, `renderSearchChips`, `updateSearchChipsVisibility`). Free text matches artist/album/title plus people/genre/label/catalog; fielded tokens (`artist:`/`album:`/`title:`/`genre:`/`year:`/`label:`/`cat:`/`credit:`/`composer:`/`producer:`/`format:`/`rate:`/`bits:`/`is:`) combine with free text and each other by AND; an unknown field name is just treated as plain text. Imports `byName`/`trackSort`/`allIdx` from `model.js`, `albumKey`/`trackIdentityKey`/`trackTier` from `tiers.js`, `emptyDetails` from `tags/index.js`, `setQueue` from `player/queue.js`, `isFavorite` from `favorites.js`, `openPersonOrArtist`/`personEntryFor`/`PERSON_ROLE_KEYS` from `credits.js`, `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js` instead of circularly from `main.js` (step 7a) (all real), and, since step 7d, `renderTracks`/`groupRow`/`songRow` from `library/view.js` instead of `main.js` — a real circular pair with it (`view.js` imports `librarySortBar`/`librarySortComparator`/`sortAlbumGroups`/`renderSearchResults` back from here).
- `src/library/view.js` (step 7d) — the "library" section's row/group renderers and selection/deletion UI helpers, plus `renderTracks()` itself. `renderTracks()` used to physically sit ~900 lines further down in `main.js`, inside the misleadingly-named "S3: gatefold album view" header (which also covered the gatefold and the sealed-record sheet, moved separately into `src/gatefold/` in step 7e — see that pair's own entries below) — purely a physical-position accident. Exports `songRow`, `groupRow`, `enterSelectMode`, `exitSelectMode`, `closeRowMenu`, `rowMenuTrackId` (a module-private `let`, exported read-only — `main.js`'s wiring only ever reads it, never reassigns it, so this doesn't trip rule 7), `openConfirmDelete`, `closeConfirmDelete`, `confirmDeleteAction` (same read-only shape as `rowMenuTrackId`), `applyTrackNumbersInOrder`, and `renderTracks`. Module-private: `artFor`, `toggleEditSelect`, `updateSelectBar`, `openRowMenu`, `togglePick`, `moveIn`, `syncQueueOrder`, `moverCtl`, `sortBar`, `renderAlbumDetail`. Since step 02 (F3 spec), playlist items and `S.albumOrder[album]` are track ids, not positions (see `library/model.js`'s own entry) — `togglePick`, the playlist picking-mode "already picked" check, the playlist's own track-list render and `renderAlbumDetail`'s manual-order render all resolve ids to indices (`idOf`/`idxOf`, now imported from `model.js`) for display, skipping — never dropping — an id whose track isn't currently present; `moverCtl`'s reorder buttons move the track within the full id-based order (its real position there, not its position among only the currently-visible rows), so a hidden/forgotten entry elsewhere in the same list keeps its own relative place. Imports `S`, `$`/`el`, `fmt` from `player/clock.js`, `setQueue` from `player/queue.js`, `trackHasCreditsContent`/`albumLinerNotes`/`albumCreditRoll`/`creditRow`/`renderPersonView` from `library/credits.js`, `isFavorite` from `library/favorites.js`, `queueSave` from `storage/meta.js`, `byName`/`groupBy`/`allIdx`/`albumTracks`/`idOf`/`idxOf` from `library/model.js`, `overlays`/`artwork` from `storage/repo.js`, `applyEdit`/`showUndoBanner` from `library/edit.js`, `countForAlbum`/`countForArtist`/`deleteAlbum`/`deleteArtist` from `library/delete.js`, `isAlbumSealed` from `library/sealed.js`, `albumKey` from `record-art/tiers.js`, `librarySortBar`/`librarySortComparator`/`sortAlbumGroups`/`renderSearchResults` from `library/search.js`, `centerEmptyState`/`renderCrate` from `crate/view.js`, `setPathNote` from `ui/path-note.js`, `updateLibTabsForLayout`/`updateHeaderActionsAvailability`/`refreshLibraryTools` from `shell/header.js`, and, since step D3, `renderSidebarPlaylists` from `shell/sidebar.js` (real, one-directional — `sidebar.js` imports nothing back) — no `main.js` import at all. `renderTracks()` calls `renderSidebarPlaylists()` as its own first line, since every `S.playlists` mutation already lands here regardless of which call site touched it — the one place the desktop sidebar's own playlists list needs to stay in sync from. This is the hub of the whole library screen, so it ends up as a real circular *pair* with six sibling modules (`library/credits.js`, `library/favorites.js`, `library/search.js`, `library/sealed.js`, `library/edit.js`, `library/delete.js`) and `crate/view.js` — each of those imports `renderTracks` (and, for three of them, `songRow`/`groupRow` too) back from here, the same peer-import shape already established between `engine.js`/`transport.js`, just repeated several times since this module is where every library-mutating action's "now redraw the screen" call lands. Since step D5, `renderTracks()` also defaults `#crateGroupToggleTab` (the new desktop-only tab-row crate-order toggle, `miziki.html`/`crate/view.js`) to hidden right after its existing `refreshLibraryTools()` call — the same "hide by default, let the branch that actually applies turn it back on" shape this function already uses for `#crumb` just below — since only the crate branch (`renderCrate()` → `crate/view.js`'s `updateCrateGroupToggleUI()`) knows whether to show it, and `renderTracks()` itself has no per-mode cleanup pass that would otherwise catch a stale "visible" state left over from a prior crate render.
- `src/gatefold/` (step 7e) — the gatefold itself and the sealed-record sheet it shares its tracklist-building code with, both of which had sat inside `main.js`'s misleadingly-named "S3: gatefold album view" header alongside the crate-shelf rendering (moved to `src/crate/view.js`, step 7c) and `renderTracks()` (moved to `src/library/view.js`, step 7d) — see each of those modules' own entries for the full history. Both files together are what the engagement's plan called "the gatefold (~1,450 lines, the biggest single piece)" before those two earlier extractions had already shrunk it to the ~640 lines that moved in this step.
  - `view.js` — the gatefold (GATEFOLD spec §A2-A7): `GF` (the ephemeral open-gatefold state, an exported `let`, read-only outside per rule 7 — `shell/routes.js` is the one external reader), `isGatefoldOpen`, `openGatefold`, `closeGatefold`, `closeGatefoldInstant`, `updateGatefoldNowPlaying`, `updateGatefoldScale`, `gatefoldSwitchPanel`, `buildGatefoldTracklist`/`gatefoldTrackIndices` (exported for `sealed-sheet.js`'s own reuse — SEALED spec §5's "the sealed sheet reuses this exact rendering"), `gatefoldSideSplits` (exported for its own unit test, same precedent as `angleDelta` in `scrub.js`, step 3c), and `GATEFOLD_ANIM` (the gatefold's own open/close timing, which had sat physically just above the old "S3" header in `main.js`, not inside it, but is gatefold code through and through — moved here alongside the functions that actually use it). Module-private: `gatefoldViewportMode`, `gatefoldNowGlyph`, `gatefoldTrackRow`, `gatefoldCreditLink`, `gatefoldSocialControlsHTML`, `buildGatefoldLeftPanel`, `gatefoldOnPlatter`, `updateGatefoldSleeveState`, `gatefoldSleeveFlourish`, `gatefoldPlayTrack`, `gatefoldRestoreRowNumber`, `gatefoldInfoAndSleeveHTML`, `gatefoldSetCoverArt`, `gatefoldTeardownGestures`, `gatefoldWireGestures`, `gatefoldSettledState`, `gatefoldSkipToSettled`, `runGatefoldOpen`.
